        ${itemsHtml}
        `; }, selectQuizOption(qId, optIdx, type) { if (window._quizSubmitted) return; if (!window._quizSelections[qId]) { window._quizSelections[qId] = []; } if (type === 'multi_mcq') { const idx = window._quizSelections[qId].indexOf(optIdx); if (idx > -1) { window._quizSelections[qId].splice(idx, 1); document.getElementById(`opt-${qId}-${optIdx}`).classList.remove('selected'); } else { window._quizSelections[qId].push(optIdx); document.getElementById(`opt-${qId}-${optIdx}`).classList.add('selected'); } } else { // Single or True/False window._quizSelections[qId] = [optIdx]; const q = window._activeQuizQuestions.find(item => item.id === qId); if (q) { q.options.forEach((_, i) => { const el = document.getElementById(`opt-${qId}-${i}`); if (el) el.classList.remove('selected'); }); } document.getElementById(`opt-${qId}-${optIdx}`).classList.add('selected'); } }, submitAssessment() { if (window._quizSubmitted) return; window._quizSubmitted = true; const questions = window._activeQuizQuestions || []; let correctCount = 0; questions.forEach(q => { const userSelected = window._quizSelections[q.id] || []; const correctIndices = q.correct_indices; const isExactMatch = userSelected.length === correctIndices.length && userSelected.every(idx => correctIndices.includes(idx)); if (isExactMatch) correctCount++; // Highlight options q.options.forEach((_, i) => { const optEl = document.getElementById(`opt-${q.id}-${i}`); if (!optEl) return; optEl.classList.remove('selected'); if (correctIndices.includes(i)) { optEl.classList.add('correct'); } else if (userSelected.includes(i)) { optEl.classList.add('incorrect'); } }); // Show explanation const expEl = document.getElementById(`exp-${q.id}`); if (expEl) { expEl.style.display = 'block'; expEl.innerHTML = ` ${isExactMatch ? '✓ Correct Answer!' : '✗ Incorrect'}
        ${q.explanation} `; } }); const scorePct = Math.round((correctCount / questions.length) * 100); const submitBtn = document.getElementById('btn-submit-quiz'); if (submitBtn) { submitBtn.style.display = 'none'; } const target = document.getElementById('quiz-active-area'); if (target) { const reportHtml = `

        Assessment Complete!

        Your Score: ${scorePct}% (${correctCount} out of ${questions.length} correct)

        `; target.insertAdjacentHTML('afterbegin', reportHtml); } }, // 8. BOOKMARKS VIEW renderBookmarks(container) { const bookmarkedIds = AppStore.getBookmarks(); let cardsHtml = ''; bookmarkedIds.forEach(id => { const info = this.findTopic(id); if (info) { cardsHtml += `
        ${info.subject_code} • Mod ${info.module_no}

        ${info.topic.title}

        ${info.topic.intro.definition}

        Study Lesson →
        `; } }); if (bookmarkedIds.length === 0) { cardsHtml = `

        No bookmarks saved yet. Click '☆ Bookmark Topic' on any lesson to save it here for fast revision.

        `; } container.innerHTML = `

        ⭐ Bookmarked Lessons (${bookmarkedIds.length})

        Quick access to lessons you have marked for examination revision.

        ${cardsHtml}
        `; }, // Helper to locate a topic across all subjects findTopic(topicId) { for (let sIdx = 0; sIdx < DATA.curriculum.subjects.length; sIdx++) { const s = DATA.curriculum.subjects[sIdx]; for (let mIdx = 0; mIdx < s.modules.length; mIdx++) { const m = s.modules[mIdx]; for (let tIdx = 0; tIdx < (m.topics || []).length; tIdx++) { if (m.topics[tIdx].id === topicId) { // Find prev & next let prev = null; let next = null; if (tIdx > 0) prev = m.topics[tIdx - 1]; if (tIdx < m.topics.length - 1) next = m.topics[tIdx + 1]; return { subject_id: s.id, subject_code: s.code, subject_name: s.name, module_no: m.module_no, topic: m.topics[tIdx], prev: prev, next: next }; } } } } return null; }, escapeHtml(str) { if (!str) return ''; return str.replace(/&/g, '&') .replace(//g, '>') .replace(/"/g, '"') .replace(/'/g, '''); }, // Global Search Modal Engine initSearch() { window.addEventListener('keydown', (e) => { if ((e.ctrlKey && e.key === 'k') || (e.key === '/' && document.activeElement.tagName !== 'INPUT')) { e.preventDefault(); this.openSearch(); } if (e.key === 'Escape') { this.closeSearch(); } }); const searchInput = document.getElementById('search-input-field'); if (searchInput) { searchInput.addEventListener('input', (e) => this.performSearch(e.target.value)); } }, openSearch() { const modal = document.getElementById('search-modal-overlay'); if (modal) { modal.classList.add('active'); const input = document.getElementById('search-input-field'); if (input) { input.value = ''; input.focus(); this.performSearch(''); } } }, closeSearch() { const modal = document.getElementById('search-modal-overlay'); if (modal) modal.classList.remove('active'); }, performSearch(query) { const list = document.getElementById('search-results-list'); if (!list) return; const q = query.trim().toLowerCase(); if (!q) { list.innerHTML = `
      2. Type keywords to search topics, model exams, and practical labs...
        3. `; return; } let results = []; // Search Topics DATA.curriculum.subjects.forEach(s => { s.modules.forEach(m => { (m.topics || []).forEach(t => { if (t.title.toLowerCase().includes(q) || t.intro.definition.toLowerCase().includes(q)) { results.push({ type: 'TOPIC', title: t.title, sub: `${s.code} • Module ${m.module_no}`, link: `#topic/${t.id}` }); } }); }); }); // Search Exams DATA.flat_exams.forEach(e => { if (e.title.toLowerCase().includes(q) || e.intro.definition.toLowerCase().includes(q)) { results.push({ type: 'EXAM', title: `Q${e.question_no}: ${e.title}`, sub: `${e.subject_id.toUpperCase()} Model Answer`, link: `#exams/${e.subject_id}` }); } }); // Search Labs ['bcac591', 'bcac592'].forEach(labId => { (DATA.practicals[labId] || []).forEach(c => { if (c.title.toLowerCase().includes(q) || c.objective.toLowerCase().includes(q)) { results.push({ type: 'LAB', title: c.title, sub: `${c.code} Lab Case Study`, link: `#practicals/${labId}` }); } }); }); if (results.length === 0) { list.innerHTML = `
      3. No results found for "${query}".
        4. `; return; } list.innerHTML = results.slice(0, 15).map(r => `
      4. ${r.type}
        ${r.title}
        ${r.sub}
        5. `).join(''); } }; // Initialize app when DOM is ready document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', () => { AppRouter.init(); });
